    HLR_pacify_south = {
        available = {
            EQC = { all_original_cores_owned_by_ROOT = yes }
            JER = { all_original_cores_owned_by_ROOT = yes }
            SIC = { all_original_cores_owned_by_ROOT = yes }
        }
        highlight_states = {
            highlight_states_trigger = {
				is_on_continent = asia
                OR = {
                    is_in_array = { JER.original_cores = THIS }
                    is_in_array = { EQC.original_cores = THIS }
                    is_in_array = { SIC.original_cores = THIS }
                }
            }
        }
        fire_only_once = yes
        complete_effect = {
            log = "[GetDateText]: [Root.GetName]: decision HLR_pacify_south"
            #Release puppets
            JER = { set_cosmetic_tag = JER_HLR }
            release_autonomy = {
				target = JER
				autonomous_state = autonomy_puppet
            }
            SIC = { set_cosmetic_tag = SIC_HLR }
            effect_tooltip = {
				release_autonomy = {
					target = SIC
					autonomous_state = autonomy_puppet
				}
            }
            hidden_effect = {
				every_state = {
					limit = {
						is_on_continent = asia
						OR = {
							is_in_array = { SIC.original_cores = THIS }
							AND = {
								is_core_of = EQC
								NOT = { is_in_array = { global.original_EQC_states = THIS } }
							}
						}
					}
					transfer_state_to = SIC
					add_core_of = SIC
				}
				set_autonomy = {
					target = SIC
					autonomous_state = autonomy_puppet
				}
            }
            EQC = { set_cosmetic_tag = EQC_HLR }
            effect_tooltip = {
				release_autonomy = {
					target = EQC
					autonomous_state = autonomy_puppet
				}
            }
            hidden_effect = {
				every_state = {
					limit = {
						is_in_array = { global.original_EQC_states = THIS }
					}
					transfer_state_to = EQC
				}
				set_autonomy = {
					target = EQC
					autonomous_state = autonomy_puppet
				}
				EQC = {
					EQC_triple_sevens = {
						add_country_leader_role = {
							promote_leader = yes
							country_leader = {
								ideology = liberalism
								expire = "1965.1.1"
							}
						}
						add_country_leader_role = {
							promote_leader = yes
							country_leader = {
								ideology = socialism
								expire = "1965.1.1"
							}
						}
						add_country_leader_role = {
							promote_leader = yes
							country_leader = {
								ideology = stratocracy
								expire = "1965.1.1"
							}
						}
					}
				}
            }
        }
        ai_will_do = {
            factor = 100
        }
    }
